Evidence: Proof1.1-2.1 normalizes by ||tv|| without v!=0. The exact total-derivative definition permits only nonzero increments. Complete the v=0 branch by the identically-zero directional quotient and linearity Df(a)0=0; retain the existing v!=0 argument.

Evidence: Statement equality holds exactly at the normalized gradient; Proof2.1 gives attainment but omits necessity. For nonzero gradient write w=gradient/||gradient||; equality for unit v gives <w,v>=1, so ||v-w||^2=2-2<w,v>=0 and norm separation gives v=w.

## lem-linear-matrix-odes-have-unique-global-solutions-on-a-given-interval

Run `physics-em-sr-40-2026-10-08`, Alpha batch-4. Published pinned mathematical supplier; raw SHA256 `993f0404d772f05ad036d289e7f40d605fe3f9cd0160605558ce0c2ebbe7b1b0`. Status: open maintenance; read-only.

Its Statement permits I=[a,b] without a<b, and asserts a differentiable solution on all of I. At a=b and n=1, the sole point is isolated, so the domain-relative derivative is undefined under `def-vector-valued-derivative-and-integral`. The exact ODE solution definition (`def-first-order-ode-initial-value-problem-and-solution`) requires differentiability on I, including every endpoint; the proof extends to an open interval and then restricts to I without noting that differentiability cannot be restricted to an isolated point.

Owned consumer: `lem-sr-fermi-walker-transport-existence-and-uniqueness`, Facts F4 and proof 2.1/3.1. It explicitly uses only nondegenerate compact intervals inside a worldline domain with at least two points. The published proof 1.1–4.1 supplies this restricted claim: extend C continuously by endpoint constants, apply local existence, use forward/backward Gronwall bounds and compact-region continuation, then restrict to a<b. This use has been independently checked; no singleton ODE claim is needed.

Repair strategy for the mathematics owner: add a<b (and the positive matrix dimension required by the ODE definition), or define the singleton case as initial data without a derivative assertion and handle it separately. Check direct mathematical consumers in their own workspace. No physics edit or certification of the published supplier is made.

- Evidence: over F2, the sign rule becomes f(v,w)=f(w,v). For f(v,w)=v1*w2 on F2^2, f(e1,e2)=1 but f(e2,e1)=0. Thus symmetry is substantive. Symmetry still need not imply alternation, as f(v,w)=v1*w1 gives f(e1,e1)=1.
- Source checked: [Conrad, Exterior Powers](https://kconrad.math.uconn.edu/blurbs/linmultialg/extmod.pdf), section 2, printed pp. 2–4, Definition 2.1, Example 2.6, Theorems 2.8 and 2.10, with their proofs.
- Disposition: confirmed false claim; maintenance remains open. `reader:1:1` is escalated in `research/physics-em-sr-40-2026-10-08-alpha-batch-1-5a-decisions.json`; no closed finding row or supplier repair is claimed.
- Repair strategy and owner decision: the mathematics owner should replace the tautology claim by the substantive symmetry condition while preserving the failure of symmetry to imply alternation. Alternatively the run owner may issue a sealed exact real-consumer context disposition while keeping this maintenance defect open. Physics imports and the original mathematics workspace were not edited.

## physics-cm-qm-gr-79-2026-10-11-step5-central-scattering-velocity-angle

Assigned draft consumer: `pthm-cm15-central-scattering`, Proof 4.1; supplier use: `def-cm15-asymptotic-data` Definition (iii), which defines signed velocity rotation as the continuous rotation of the velocity direction. The endpoint identity with R_(2Δ−π) alone determines that rotation only modulo 2π.

Local repair: the polar velocity has positive tangential component J/(μρ), so ψ=arccos(ρ′/|r′|) lies in (0,π) and θ+ψ is a continuous real velocity angle. Its incoming and outgoing limits are θ_-+π and θ_+, yielding net rotation 2Δ−π. Existing read-only mathematical suppliers `thm-sine-and-cosine-derivatives`, `thm-quarter-turn-values-and-shift-formulas`, `thm-sine-cosine-signs-monotonicity-and-ranges`, and `thm-continuous-inverse` justify the inverse cosine and its endpoint continuity; no defect or repair to those suppliers is claimed.

Status: locally repaired and reviewed by the assigned repair author; no independent judgment or certification asserted. Statement, physical_scope and empirical qualifications are unchanged, so this proof-only correction creates no consumer-interface event. Owning batch-31 contract and manifest record the actual prerequisites. Source actually consulted: Fitzpatrick §6.4, equations (340)–(351), https://farside.ph.utexas.edu/teaching/336k/Newtonhtml/node51.html. The same step correctly checks the tensor generators by bilinearity, so the descent and consumed product-basis result remain adequate; this is nonfatal terminology, not a tensor-construction counterexample. Published bytes remain read-only. Owner strategy: replace that phrase by “C-bilinear”; retain the explicit generator calculation and current missing-publication-evidence hold.

Published item: `lem-qsm-dt-stationary-variance-integral-identity`; exact location Proof 2.1. Owned consumer: `thm-qsmx-conditional-diffusive-green-kubo` via `thm-qsm-dt-variance-green-kubo`. Declared suppliers: `def-c-star-algebra` and `lem-cm-integral-and-ftc-for-continuous-normed-space-curves`. Neither supplies two-variable Fubini/triangle slicing. Current published proof and supplier statements independently read.

Classification: nonfatal omitted deduction; OPEN published-owner maintenance, run-local disposition nonfatal-recorded. Complete local closure: for h=T/n and continuous f(u)=phi(V_a alpha_u(V_b)), stationarity converts the finite product Riemann sum into h*T*f(0)+h*sum_{k=1}^{n-1}(T-k*h)*(f(k*h)+f(-k*h)), since each difference k occurs n-|k| times. Passing to one-variable Riemann limits proves the two weighted slice integrals without a product-region theorem. Hermiticity then gives the symmetrized correlation; positivity gives the covariance matrix sign. This validates the exact scalar use in the owned consumer without certifying any publication/audit marker.

Repair strategy: published owner inserts that grid calculation, or declares exact Fubini/substitution prerequisites and proves triangular-cutoff integrability. Published carrier remains read-only. ## physics-non-fluid-129-2026-10-21-r2-3a-batch-127-published-right-annihilation

Published supplier: `lem-temperley-lieb-jones-wenzl-partial-trace-and-uniqueness`; owned consumer: `prop-tgrx-tl-projector-entropy` (also cited in `def-tgrx-tl-boundary-representation`). Original finding: `reader:127:1`; closed scoped defect row: `physics-non-fluid-129-2026-10-21-r2-3a-batch-127-published-right-annihilation`. Status: OPEN published mathematics-owner maintenance; `nonfatal-recorded` does not repair or audit the published source.

Published step 2.1 omits reflection invariance from its induction assertions. With P=P_r, Q=p_(r-1) tensor 1 tensor 1, lambda=[r+1]/[r], c=1/lambda, the recursion gives PQ=QP=P. The written bPb=lambda Qb implies (P-cPbP)b=Pb-cP(bPb)=Pb-c lambda PQb=0. Thus right annihilation follows by elementary algebra. Idempotence and partial/closure trace used by the owned consumer are valid independently. Published source remains read-only; recorded nonfatal maintenance with its exact repair strategy.

Exact suppliers: the unit-denominator recursion, quantum-integer recurrence, diagram local relations and closure-component identities listed in that lemma. Source comparison: Joshua Chen, https://arxiv.org/pdf/1502.06845, Theorem 2.3.2 and Proposition 2.3.6, printed pp. 12–16 (PDF pp. 20–24). Repair strategy: insert the displayed algebraic calculation in step 2.1, leaving all interfaces unchanged; alternatively establish reflection invariance by induction. No outside edit is made. Run `physics-non-fluid-129-2026-10-21-r2`, group `batch-122`. These are distinct proof omissions from the publication-marker issue already indexed for the spectral supplier. Both original findings are confirmed nonfatal and recorded without editing published carriers. Reports and exact ledger decisions: `research/physics-non-fluid-129-2026-10-21-r2-alpha-batch-122-3a.md` and its `-decisions.json` companion.

- `reader:122:1`, `lem-qsm-matrix-state-calculus`, Proof 5.1; defect `pnf129-r2-b122-spectral-extreme-midpoint`. Owned consumer `lem-tgrx-finite-matrix-entropy-variation` uses only the supplier's spectral decomposition and functional calculus, proved in 2.1–3.1. Missing witnesses: for two positive eigenvalues, choose 0<delta<min(p_i,p_j) and define rho_±=rho±delta(P_i-P_j). Each has nonnegative eigenvalues and trace one; they are distinct and average to rho. Owner strategy: insert this explicit construction without changing the Statement. Published maintenance remains OPEN; the owned-use disposition is nonfatal-recorded.
- `reader:122:2`, `thm-it-quantum-relative-entropy-partial-trace-data-processing`, Proof 1.2; defect `pnf129-r2-b122-dpi-cross-trace`. Owned consumer `prop-tgrx-finite-modular-balance` applies finite partial-trace monotonicity to nonnegative density operators. On supp(D) tensor H_B, log(D tensor I_B/d)=(log D) tensor I_B-(log d)I. Finite product-basis summation gives tr((C tensor I_B/d)((log D) tensor I_B))=tr(C log D), irrespective of commutation; the identical -(log d)tr C terms cancel in relative entropy. Zero and failed-support branches remain valid. Owner strategy: insert this short factorization without changing the Statement. The rest of the current Weyl-twirl, convexity and isometric-invariance proof was checked. Watrous §5.2.3 Theorem 5.35 (https://jhwatrous.github.io/TQI.pdf, printed pp.280–281) corroborates the result; the natural-log version follows by multiplying the base-two inequality by ln 2. Published maintenance remains OPEN; the owned-use disposition is nonfatal-recorded.

Neither disposition supplies a publication audit or claims a published repair. Original mathematical suppliers and imported bytes remain read-only.

Additional unowed alert: `fluid-dynamics-all-47-2026-10-23-3a-batch-23-published-burgers-initial-support`: Published Burgers proof step 1.2 and step 4.1 wrongly confine the difference from the initial step to [u_L epsilon,u_R epsilon]. For u_L=1,u_R=2 and 0<x<epsilon the profile is 1 and the datum 2. Enlarge to [min(0,u_L epsilon),max(0,u_R epsilon)] of length at most (|u_L|+|u_R|)epsilon; boundedness still proves convergence. Local Riemann step 4.2 and Burgers fan use the correct estimate, so no owned conclusion is blocked. Published owner repair remains required.

Repair strategy: original mathematics owner moves inline links after the complete original heading, preserving all mathematical hypotheses, dependencies, attribution and publication status; correct the Burgers support estimate as above and review its consumers. Reconcile imports only through owner controls.
